[\[78b.2\]]rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/shes rab pha rol phyin pa la/_/de'i rgyan tshig le'ur byas pa dag_/kun gyi rgyan du 'gyur bar ni/_/rnam par dbye phyir gus phyag 'tshal/_/'gro la chags pas kun chags mdzad/_/'phags pa 
[\[78b.3\]]thogs med skyob nyid kyis/_/mi pham mgon la gsan nas ni/_/bstan bcos chen po'i rnam bshad mdzad/_/'gro don rtsa lag dbyig gi gnyen/_/rang gi mos pa gtsor byed pas/_/shes bya nang gi yin pa la/_/yang dag rten [#]nas rnam par bkrol/_/'phags 
[\[78b.4\]]pa'i khongs su gtogs pa yi/_/rnam par grol ba zhes byas kyang /_/des [#]byas byas pa min mthong nas/_/dbu mar gnas pa'i blos rnam phye/_/de yi 'og tu rnam grol sde/_/dad pa'i sa la gnas pa yis/_/bstan bcos mtha' dag ma rnyed par/_/rang dang 
[\[78b.5\]]rjes [#]mthun rnam bshad byas/_/de ltar mkhas pas snang byas shing /_/'ga' zhig kho nas ma rnyed pa'i/_/bstan bcos mtha' dag 'di de ltar/_/rnyed pa gang de ya mtshan no/_/de ltas shin tu rnyed dka' ba[#]/_/zab mo'i lam 'di ci zhig ltar/_/sangs 
[\[78b.6\]]rgyas dbang gis rnyed pa ni/_/mkhas pa rnams kyis dbye ba'i rigs/_/rnam pa kun du bdag gi ni/_/spyod yul min yang bsod nams dag_/bsgrub [#]dang mthun phyir rang gzhan la/_/phan gdags 'dod pas spro ba skyes/_/'phags pa byams pa rang nyid dam 
[\[78b.7\]]pa'i spyod pa dang mthun par rab tu ston pas/_so so rang gi [#]rig pa'i ye shes kyis [#]shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i yul can gyi rab tu dang ba ni legs pa thams cad 'thob pa'i rgyu'i gtso bo yin no zhes bya bar nges par bzung nas/_gzhan dag bcom ldan 'das ma yon tan rin 
[\[79a\]]
[\[79a.1\]]po che [#]phul du dbyung du med cing dpag tu med pa'i 'byung gnas la don gyis 'jug par bya ba'i phyir rab tu dang ba bskyed pa'i ched du/_re zhig dang por yum la yon tan ji lta ba bzhin du brjod pa sngon du 'gro ba can gyi phyag 'tshal ba/_/nyan thos zhi ba tshol rnams kun 
[\[79a.2\]]shes nyid kyis nyer zhir khrid mdzad gang yin dang /_/'gro la phan par byed rnams lam shes nyid kyis 'jig rten don sgrub mdzad pa gang /_/gang dang yang dag ldan pas thub rnams rnam pa kun ldan sna tshogs 'di gsungs pa[#]/_/nyan thos byang chub sems dpa'i [#]tshogs bcas 
[\[79a.3\]]sangs rgyas kyi ni yum de la phyag 'tshal/_/zhes bya ba gsungs so/_/tshigs su bcad pa 'dis ni 'di'i che ba'i bdag nyid phul du byung ba dang ldan pa [#]brjod de/_de thos nas re zhig dad pa'i [#]rjes su 'brang ba rnams ni 'di la the tshom med par myur du rab tu dad pa skye'o/_/chos 
[\[79a.4\]]kyi rjes su 'brang ba rnams kyang gcig dang du ma'i ngo bo nyid dang bral ba'i phyir zhes bya ba la sogs pa'i tshad mas gzhi dang lam dang rnam pa skye ba med par yongs su shes pa tshigs su bcad pa'i don gyi mtshan nyid kyi [#]yum la gnod pa ma mthong nas/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad 
[\[79a.5\]]mkhyen pa nyid gsum gyi bdag nyid [#]tshul gsum pa can/_sangs rgyas la sogs pa bskrun [#]par mdzad pa ni gdon mi za bar srid pa nyid de [#]zhes nges par gzung ste/_de la rab tu dang ba skyed par byed pa nyid do/_/de la rab tu dang bas kyang de'i yon tan mngon par 'dod pa'i gang zag gnyis 
[\[79a.6\]]ka yang de dang de la brten pa'i gsung rab la rnam pa thams cad du gzung ba la sogs pa'i don du shin tu gus par byed do/_/de nas thos pa las byung ba la sogs pa'i shes pa skye ba'i rim gyis legs pa'i mchog 'thob [#]par 'gyur ro/_/de'i phyir bcom ldan 'das ma la rab tu dang 
[\[79a.7\]]ba ni legs pa thams cad 'thob [#]pa'i rgyu'i gtso bo yin no/_/de la thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gsum gyis ni don brgyad bsdus la de dag kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yin zhing des kyang 'chad par 'gyur ba'i tshul gyis don thams cad phun sum tshogs pa yin no zhes de ltar thugs 
[\[79b\]]
[\[79b.1\]]la bzhag nas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gsum gyi sgo nas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bstod do/_/nyan thos dang de'i phyogs su bslan [#]pa rnam par thar pa cung zad tsam zhig gis lhag pa rim pa gcig gis bstan pa rang sangs rgyas kyang ste/_mya ngan las 'da' bar 'dod 
[\[79b.2\]]pa rnams gzhi thams cad skye ba med par yongs su shes pa nyid kyis phung po dang bcas pa dang phung po med pa'i mtshan nyid kyis [#]mya ngan las 'das pa rnam pa gnyis thob par mdzad pa gang yin pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' 'khor ba ji srid par 'gro ba'i don byed pa rnams lam 
[\[79b.3\]]thams cad skye ba med par rtogs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis 'gro ba mtha' dag gi don sgrub [#]par mdzad pa gang yin pa dang /_rnam pa thams cad skye ba med par thugs su chud pa gang dang ldan na/_sangs rgyas yang dag par bsdams pa'i sku can rnal 'byor can gyi dbang phyug gi gtso bo rnams spyod pa'i 
[\[79b.4\]]gnyen por chos kyi 'khor lo ma lus pa rnam pa thams cad bskor [#]bar mdzad pa/_sangs rgyas nyan thos la sogs pas yongs su bskor ba de sgrub par byed pa nyid kyi yul [#]de la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de ltar phyag 'tshal ba dang bstod pa sngon du 'gro ba'i bstan bcos mngon par rtogs 
[\[79b.5\]]pa'i rgyan gzhi dang gnyen po dang rnam pa rnams las gang yang rung ba zhig bsdus pa mdzad na/_phyogs dang po ltar na ni 'bad pa don med pa yin te/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di las ni mtshan nyid kyi bstan bcos dag las mi smos pa'i gzhi gang yin pa de 'byung ba ni med do/_/
[\[79b.6\]]gnyis pa ltar na yang rnam par byang ba'i dngos po bsdus pa'i phyir kun nas nyon mongs pa'i dngos po ma bsdus pas 'di gang gi gnyen po yin pa khong du chud par mi 'gyur ro/_/gsum pa ltar na yang rnam pa gzhi [#]med pa tsam zhig bsdus pa'i phyir don rtogs pa [#]med pas/_cung zad 
[\[79b.7\]]kyang ma bshad pa'i phyir don med pa yin no snyam du gzhan dag sems par 'gyur ba ma yin nam zhe na/_de ni ma yin te/_gang gi phyir nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang /_bla na med pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis [#{]go rims [,]go rim [}]ji lta ba bzhin thams cad mkhyen 
[\[80a\]]
[\[80a.1\]]pa nyid gsum gyis mngon par rtogs pa mtha' dag bsdus pas na phyogs gsum car gyis kyang bsdus pa'i phyir ro/_/'di ltar rgyal ba'i yum bar ma las rab 'byor thams cad shes pa nyid ni nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams kyi yin no/_/lam shes 
[\[80a.2\]]pa nyid ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yin no/_/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yin no/_/bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du thams cad 'tshal ba nyid nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams kyi lags/_
[\[80a.3\]]rab 'byor thams cad ni phyi dang nang gi chos rnams ji snyed pa 'di tsam yin la/_de dag kyang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams kyis shes kyi/_lam thams cad kyis kyang ma yin la/_rnam pa thams cad du yang ma yin pas de'i phyir nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas rnams kyi ni 
[\[80a.4\]]thams cad shes pa nyid ces bya'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du lam gyi rnam pa 'tshal ba nyid byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lags/_rab 'byor byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis ni nyan thos kyi lam gang yin pa dang rang sangs rgyas kyi lam gang yin pa dang sangs rgyas 
[\[80a.5\]]kyi lam gang yin pa'i lam thams cad bskyed par bya zhing lam thams cad shes par bya'o/_/de dag kyang yongs su rdzogs par bya zhing de dag gis [#]lam gyi bya ba yang bya la/_smon lam yongs su rdzogs par ma byas pa dang sems can rnams yongs su smin par ma byas pa 
[\[80a.6\]]dang sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su ma sbyangs par de dag gis [#]yang dag pa'i mtha' mngon sum du ni mi bya'o/_/de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ni lam gyi rnam pa [#]shes pa nyid ces bya'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ci'i slad du rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid de bzhin gshegs 
[\[80a.7\]]pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi lags/_rab 'byor rnam pa gang dag dang rtags gang dag dang mtshan ma gang dag gis chos rnams rab tu brjod pa'i rnam pa de dag dang rtags de dag dang mtshan ma de dag de bzhin gshegs pas thugs su chud de de'i phyir de 
[\[80b\]]
[\[80b.1\]]bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ces bya'o/_/de bzhin du mdor bsdus pa las kyang nyan thos kyi sa la slob par 'dod pas kyang zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs la/_rgyas pa las kyang rgya cher gsungs 
[\[80b.2\]]so/_/de la thams cad shes pa nyid ni gzugs la sogs pa'i chos la mi rtag pa la sogs pa'i rten can 'bras bu bdag tu rmongs pa spangs pa can yin no/_/lam shes pa nyid ni theg pa thams cad kyis nges par 'byung ba dang /_de kho na nyid mngon sum du mi byed pa'i rten 
[\[80b.3\]]can 'bras bu sems can ma bsdus pa sdud [#]pa la sogs par byed pa can yin no/_/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni chos thams cad skye ba med pa'i rten can/_'bras bu nam mkha'i khams kyi [#]mthas gtugs par sems can gyi don rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /_yang dag pa nyid 
[\[80b.4\]]kyi mtha' rtogs pa dang /_bag chags kyis [#]mtshams sbyor ba nyid spangs pa can yin [#]no/_/de lta bas na mngon par rtogs pa'i rgyan ni gzhi dang gnyen po dang rnam pa thams cad [#]bsdus pas mngon par rtogs pa ril rdzogs par bstan pa yin no/_/zhes bya ba 'di 'thad pa yin 
[\[80b.5\]]no/_/skye bo blo gros dman pa dag bcom ldan 'das rgyas pa dang 'bring dang mdor bsdus pa la dad pa'i sems can la phan 'dogs par bzhed pa thugs rje can nyid kyis rgyal ba'i yum de dang de las mngon par rtogs pa brgyad kyi rim pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i don mtha' 
[\[80b.6\]]dag gsal bar mdzad pa ma yin nam/_bcom ldan 'das 'phags pa ma rgyal ba las rgyal bas yang de bstan pa la don ci zhig yod snyam du dogs pa bsal ba'i phyir/_don la the tshom bskyed pa nyid kyis 'jug pa'i yan lag rang gi bstan bcos kyi brjod par bya ba dang /_

[\[81a\]]
[\[81a.1\]]blo dang ldan pas mthong 'gyur phyir/_/bde blag tu ni rtogs pa zhes/_/bya ba rtsom pa'i dgos pa yin/_/zhes bya ba yin no/_/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni sangs rgyas nyid kyi lam [#]ste mngon par rtogs pa thams cad nye bar mtshon pa lhur 
[\[81a.2\]]len pa yin no/_/bcom ldan 'das cho 'phrul rnam pa gsum gyis 'gro ba mtha' dag la rjes su ston par mdzad pa de nyid kyis rgyal ba'i yum rnam pa gsum car las brjod par bya ba nyid du bstan pa gang yin pa[#]/_rjod par byed pa dang brjod par bya ba dag thabs dang thabs 
[\[81a.3\]]kyis bsgrub par bya ba'i tshul gyis 'brel par mdzad pa phyi rol pa dang cig shos kyi 'dod chags dang bral ba la sogs pa chos thams cad bdag med pa la goms par ma byas pa rnams kyis thos pa las byung ba la sogs pa'i shes pa skye ba'i rim gyis ma myangs pa ste/_byang chub 
[\[81a.4\]]sems dpa' rang dang gzhan gyi don du [#]phun sum tshogs pa sgrub pa ci la yang ma rag par chas pa dag kho nas nyams su myong ba shin tu gsal bas bzhag pa'i bag chags las byung ba'i dran pa'i shes pa la/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mdo'i don [#]ma lus pa mngon par 
[\[81a.5\]]rtogs pa brgyad kyi bdag nyid byang chub kyi sems dang sbyin pa la sogs pa sgrub [#]pa'i spyod pa legs par bzhag nas/_chos kyi dbyings kun du 'gro ba rtogs pa'i mtshan nyid sa rab tu dga' ba la sogs pa rtogs pa'i rim pas rnam pa thams cad mngon sum du byed par 'gyur ba zhes 
[\[81a.6\]]bya ba dgos [#]pa'i yang dgos pa'i yang [#]ched du mngon par 'dod pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i don byang chub kyi sems dang sgrub pa la sogs pa'i mtshan nyid gdul bya rnams kyis bde blag tu rtogs par 'gyur ba ni bstan bcos rtsom pa'i dgos pa yin no/_/de ltar 'brel 
[\[81a.7\]]pa la sogs pa bsnyad nas/_gdul bya rnams kyis bde blag tu rtogs par bya ba'i phyir dang /_bstan bcos kyi don rnam par dbye ba byas pa yang ma 'dzings pa nyid kyis bshad pa [#]bya sla bar gzigs nas tshig le'ur byas pa bco lngas mdo dang rgyas par bstan pas shes rab kyi 
[\[81b\]]
[\[81b.1\]]pha rol tu phyin pa'i lus rnam par bzhag pa gsungs te/_shes rab pha rol phyin pa ni/_/dngos po brgyad kyis yang dag bshad/_/rnam kun mkhyen nyid lam shes nyid/_/de nas thams cad shes pa nyid/_/rnam kun mngon rdzogs rtogs pa dang /_/rtse mor phyin dang 
[\[81b.2\]]mthar gyis pa/_/skad cig gcig mngon rdzogs byang chub/_/chos kyi sku dang de rnam brgyad/_/sems bskyed pa dang gdams ngag dang /_/nges 'byed yan lag rnam bzhi dang /_/sgrub pa yi ni rten gyur pa/_/chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin dang /_/dmigs pa dag dang ched 
[\[81b.3\]]dang ni/_/go cha 'jug pa'i bya ba dang /_/tshogs ni nges par 'byung bcas rnams/_/thub pa'i rnam kun mkhyen pa nyid/_/mog mog por byed la sogs dang /_/slob ma bse ru'i lam gang dang /_/'di dang gzhan pa'i yon tan gyis/_/phan yon che ba mthong ba'i lam/_/
[\[81b.4\]]byed pa dang ni mos pa dang /_/bstod dang bkur dang bsngags pa dang /_/bsngo dang rjes su yid rang ba/_/yid la byed pa bla med dag_/sgrub dang shin tu dag pa zhes/_/bya ba 'di ni bsgom pa'i lam/_/byang chub sems dpa' mkhas rnams kyi/_/lam shes nyid ni de 'drar 
[\[81b.5\]]bshad/_/shes pas srid la mi gnas shing /_/snying rjes [#]zhi la mi gnas dang /_/thabs ma yin pas ring ba dang /_/thabs kyis ring ba ma yin nyid/_/mi mthun gnyen po'i phyogs dag dang /_/sbyor dang de yi mnyam nyid dang /_/nyan thos la sogs mthong ba'i 
[\[81b.6\]]lam/_/de 'dra kun shes nyid du 'dod/_/rnam pa sbyor bar bcas rnams dang /_/yon tan skyon ni mtshan nyid bcas/_/thar dang nges 'byed cha mthun [#]dang /_/slob pa phyir mi ldog pa'i tshogs/_/srid dang zhi ba mnyam nyid dang /_/zhing dag bla na med pa ni/_/rnam kun mngon 
[\[81b.7\]]rdzogs rtogs pa ste/_/'di ni thabs mkhas bcas pa yin/_/de yi rtags dang rnam 'phel dang /_/brtan dang sems kun gnas pa dang /_/mthong zhes bya dang bsgom pa zhes/_/bya ba'i lam ni so so la/_/rnam par rtog pa rnam bzhi yi/_/gnyen po rnam pa

[\[82a.1\]]bzhi dang ni/_/bar chad med pa'i ting nge 'dzin/_/log par bsgrub [#]dang bcas pa ni/_/rtse mo'i mngon rtogs mthar gyis pa/_/rnam gsum rnam bcu skad cig ma/_/gcig gis mngon rdzogs byang chub pa/_/mtshan nyid kyis ni rnam pa bzhi/_/
[\[82a.2\]]ngo bo nyid longs rdzogs bcas dang /_/de bzhin gzhan pa sprul pa ni/_/chos sku mdzad pa dang bcas pa/_/rnam pa bzhir [#]ni yang dag brjod/_/ces bya ba yin no/_/de la tshig le'ur byas pa dang po gnyis kyis ni dngos po brgyad bsdus pa'i phyir mdor bstan pa yin no/_/
[\[82a.3\]]tshig le'ur byas pa bcu gsum gyis ni de dag nyid kyi don bsdus nas rgyas par bshad de/_/de ltar na bsdus pa dang rgyas par bshad pa'i phyir legs par bshad pa yin no/_/bsdus pa'i don gyi tshig le'ur byas pa rnams ni/_bstan bcos mdzad pa nyid kyis/_sems 
[\[82a.4\]]bskyed pa ni gzhan don phyir/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa 'chad par 'gyur ba'i bstan bcos mtha' dag gis 'chad pas/_de dag rnam par bshad pas bshad par bsams te/_zlos pa'i nyes par 'gyur du dogs nas rnam par ma bkrol [#]lo/_/de ltar bsdus pa'i don 
[\[82a.5\]]kun bstan nas/_byang chub sems dpa' byang chub rjes su 'thob [#]par 'dod pas 'bras bu yin pa'i phyir rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob par bya dgos pas/_dang por rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid de sdud pa'i tshig le'ur byas pa rnam par bshad pa'i sgo nas bshad par 
[\[82a.6\]]bzhed de/_sems bskyed pa rang gi ngo bo dmigs pa dang bcas pa/_sems bskyed pa ni gzhan don phyir/_/yang dag rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod/_/ces gsungs te/_sangs rgyas su grub nas gzhan gyi don gyi phyir skal ba ji lta ba bzhin du 'bad par bya'o zhes 
[\[82a.7\]]sems bskyed pa gzhan gyi don du yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod pa'i mtshan nyid smon pa dang 'jug pa'i ngo bo nyid rnam pa gnyis so/_/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod pa nyid ni de don du gnyer ba dge ba'i chos la 'dun pa sems las byung ba yin la/_sems 
[\[82b\]]
[\[82b.1\]]bskyed pa ni sems yul khyad par du 'phags pa snang ba can 'byung ba ma yin nam/_ji ltar na de sems bskyed pa yin par 'gyur zhe na bden mod kyi/_'on kyang 'dir dge ba'i chos la 'dun pa'i mtshan nyid don du gnyer ba yod na byang chub kyi sems bskyed pas rgyus [(]'bas [,]'bras [)]
[\[82b.2\]]bu bstan pa yin te/_de ltar don du gnyer ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i chos thams cad 'phel bar 'gyur ro zhes shes par bya ba'i phyir nye bar btags pa la rten [#]pa yin pas nyes pa med do/_/rnam pa gcig tu na smon lam ni don du gnyer ba ste/_yang dag par rdzogs 
[\[82b.3\]]pa'i byang chub 'dod pa nyid yin la/_de dang lhan cig spyod pa'i sems bskyed pa de'i sgrar bstan pa ni de ltar na [#]byang chub sems dpa' rnams la smon lam dang ldan pa'i sems de skye'o zhes bya bar shes par bya ba'i phyir yin no/_/yang gang [#]'dod pa'i bdag nyid dang /_gang gi don du 
[\[82b.4\]]sems bskyed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub de ni gang yin gzhan gyi don ni gang yin zhe na/_de dang de ni mdo bzhin du/_/bsdus dang rgyas pa'i sgo nas brjod/_/ces bya ba gsungs te[#]/_rgyal ba'i yum rnam pa gsum car las kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /_sbyin 
[\[82b.5\]]pa la sogs pa thams cad la sbyin par bya ba dang /_sbyin pa po dang len pa po la sogs pa mi dmigs par bsgrub [#]par bya'o zhes shes par byed pa'i don can dang /_sems can thams cad skal ba ji lta ba bzhin du mya ngan las 'das pa dang /_ser sna can la sogs pa rnams 
[\[82b.6\]]sbyin pa la sogs pa la dgod par 'dod pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid la bsgrub par bya'o zhes bya ba la sogs pa rab tu ston pa lhur byed pa'i ngag gis mdo'i don dang mi 'gal bar bsdus pa dang rgyas pa'i sgo nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub dang gzhan gyi don 
[\[82b.7\]]bstan te/_de ltar na sems bskyed pa ni gzhan gyi don gyi phyir yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod pa'i bdag nyid yin par shes par bya'o/_/gtsug lag gi gzhung skabs kyi don ston pa lhur len pa ni thams cad las kyang gsungs mod kyi tshig [#]mangs su dogs nas 
[\[83a\]]
[\[83a.1\]]khungs nyid du ma bris so/_/de ltar sems bskyed pa dmigs pa dang rang gi ngo bo dang bcas pa brjod nas/_da ni de'i rab tu dbye ba rnam pa nyi shu rtsa gnyis bar skabs kyi tshigs su bcad pa/_de yang sa gser zla ba me/_/gter dang rin chen 'byung 
[\[83a.2\]]gnas mtsho/_/rdo rje ri sman bshes gnyen dang /_/yid bzhin nor bu nyi ma glu/_/rgyal po mdzod dang lam po che/_/bzhon [#]pa bkod ma'i chu dang ni/_/sgra snyan chu bo sprin rnams kyis/_/rnam pa nyi shu rtsa gnyis so/_/zhes bya ba dag gis bshad de/_'dun pa dang /_bsam pa

[\[92a.6\]]ngoms nyid dang ni/_/zang zing med par chos sbyin dang /_/sangs rgyas zhing kun sbyong ba dang /_/'khor bas yongs su mi skyo dang /_/ngo tsha khrel yod ces bya ba/_/rlom med bdag nyid de rnam lnga/_zhes bya ba ni/_dam pa'i chos mnyan pas ngoms mi shes pa dang /_
[\[92a.7\]]rnyed pa la sogs pa la mi lta ba'i sems kyis dam pa'i chos rab tu ston pa dang /_rang gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing rten dang brten [#]pa kun du sbyong ba dang /_phan btags pa'i sems can mi mthun pa la sogs pa mthong bas yongs su mi skyo ba dang /_rang dang gzhan la ltos [#]nas mi 
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]dge ba'i chos mi byed pa zhes bya ba de lta bu'i yongs su sbyong ba rnam pa lnga po/_/snga ma bzhin du ngo bo nyid du mi rtog pas yongs su sbyang ba byas pas sa gsum pa 'od byed pa rtogs pa yin no/_/nags gnas 'dod chung chog shes dang /_/sbyangs pa yang dag sdom bsten [#]dang /_/
[\[92b.2\]]bslab pa yongs su mi gtong dang /_/'dod pa rnams la smod pa dang /_/mya ngan 'das dang bdog kun gtong /_/mi zhum pa dang lta ba med/_/ces bya ba ni/_dgon par gnas pa dang /_rnyed pa ma thob pa la mngon par 'dod pa med pa dang /_rnyed pa thob pa la lhag par 
[\[92b.3\]]don du gnyer ba med pa dang /_bsod snyoms pa nyid la sogs pa sbyangs pa'i yon tan yang dag par sdom pa dang /_bslab pa blangs pa srog la sogs pa'i phyir yang yongs su mi gtong ba dang /_'dod pa'i yon tan la nyes dmigs su lta bas smod pa dang /_gdul bya 
[\[92b.4\]]dang 'tsham par mya ngan las 'das pa la gzhol bar byed pa dang /_bdog pa thams cad yongs su gtong ba dang /_dge ba sgrub pa la sems mi zhum pa dang /_dngos po thams cad la mi lta ba zhes bya ba de lta bu'i yongs su sbyong ba snga ma lta bu rnam pa bcus sa bzhi pa 'od 'phro 
[\[92b.5\]]ba can gnon pa yin no/_/'dris dang khyim la 'khren pa dang /_/'du 'dzir gyur pa'i gnas dang ni/_/bdag bstod gzhan la brnyas pa dang /_/mi dge'i las kyi lam bcu dang /_/rlom pas khengs dang phyin ci log_/blo ngan dang ni nyon mongs bzod/_/bcu po 
[\[92b.6\]]'di ni rnam [#]spangs na/_/sa lnga pa ni yang dag 'thob/_/ces bya ba ni/_rnyed pa la sogs pa'i don du rab tu byung ba la sogs pa dang 'grogs pa dang /_dad pa can gyi khyim nye bar mi ston pa dang /_skye bo mang po'i grong la sogs pa dang /_bdag la bsngags pa dang /_
[\[92b.7\]]gzhan la smod pa dang /_mi dge ba'i las kyi lam bcu dang /_thos pa la sogs pas rlom zhing gzhan la mi 'dud [#]pa dang /_dge ba dang mi dge ba la [#]bzlog ste mngon par zhen pa dang /_log par lta ba la sogs pa blo gros ngan pa dang /_'dod chags la sogs pa 
[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]nyon mongs pa thams cad la phyogs par byed pa zhes bya ba de lta bu'i mtshan nyid kyi chos bcu rnam par spong zhing bzlog pa'i chos don gyis 'phangs pa yongs su sbyong ba rnam pa bcus snga ma bzhin du sa lnga pa sbyang dka' ba 'thob pa yin no/_/sbyin dang tshul khrims bzod 
[\[93a.2\]]brtson 'grus/_/bsam gtan shes rab rab rdzogs pas/_/slob ma dang ni bse ru la/_/dga' dang skrag pa'i sems spangs pa/_/bslangs [#]na mi zhum bdog pa kun/_/btang yang mi dga' med pa dang /_/dbul yang slong ba mi spong bas/_/sa ni drug pa yang dag 'thob/_/
[\[93a.3\]]ces bya ba ni/_/pha rol tu phyin pa drug yongs su rdzogs pas nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas la 'dod pa dang /_ngo bo nyid mi dmigs pa la skrag pa dang /_slong ba po'i skye bos bslangs [#]na zhum pa dang /_bdog pa thams cad rang gi ngang gis gtong bar zhugs pa la mi dga' ba dang /_
[\[93a.4\]]bkren yang slong ba'i skye bo spong ba'i sems spong ba ste/_de lta bu'i yongs su sbyong ba bcu gnyis kyis snga ma bzhin du sa drug pa mngon du gyur pa kun shes par 'gyur ba yin no/_/bdag dang sems can 'dzin pa dang /_/srog dang gang zag chad rtag dang /_/mtshan ma rgyu dang phung po 
[\[93a.5\]]dang /_khams dang skye mched dag dang ni/_/khams gsum po la gnas dang [#]chags/_/sems ni kun du zhum pa dang /_/dkon mchog gsum dang tshul khrims la/_/der lta ba yi mngon zhen dang /_/stong pa nyid la rtsod pa dang /_/de dang 'gal ba'i nyes pa ni/_/nyi shu gang 
[\[93a.6\]]la rnam chad pa/_/de yis sa ni bdun pa 'thob/_/ces bya ba ni/_bdag dang /_sems can dang /_srog dang /_gang zag dang /_chad pa dang /_rtag pa dang /_mtshan ma dang /_rgyu dang /_phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched dang /_khams gsum pa la gnas pa dang /_
[\[93a.7\]]chags pa dang /_sems kun du zhum pa dang /_sangs rgyas dang /_chos dang /_dge 'dun dang /_tshul khrims su lta ba'i mngon par zhen pa dang /_stong pa nyid la rtsod pa dang /_'gal ba rjod [#]pa 'dzin pa yongs su spangs pa ste/_nyes pa de lta bu nyi shu dang bral nas/_bzlog pa'i 
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]chos don gyis 'phangs pa yongs su sbyong ba rnam pa nyi shus snga ma bzhin du sa bdun pa ring du song ba kun shes par 'gyur ba yin no/_/don gyis [#]'phangs pa'i chos nyid bstan pa'i phyir/_rnam thar sgo gsum shes pa dang /_/'khor gsum rnam par dag nyid dang /_/snying rje dang ni 
[\[93b.2\]]rlom med dang /_/chos mnyam nyid dang tshul gcig shes/_/mi skye ba dang bzod shes dang /_/chos rnams rnam pa gcig tu ston/_/rtog pa kun du 'joms pa dang /_/'du shes lta dang nyon mongs spong /_/zhi gnas nges par sems pa [#]dang /_/lhag mthong la ni mkhas pa 
[\[93b.3\]]dang /_/sems dul ba dang thams cad la/_/thogs pa med pa'i ye shes dang /_/chags pa'i sa min gar 'dod par/_/zhing gzhan du ni mnyam 'gro dang /_/kun du bdag gi ngo bo ni/_/ston pa nyid dang nyi shu'o/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa 
[\[93b.4\]]nyid dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_smon pa med pa yang dag par shes pa dang /_dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam la gsad [#]par bya ba dang /_gsod pa po dang /_bsad pa mi dmigs pa la sogs pa dang /_skye bo thams cad la dmigs pa'i snying brtse ba dang /_dngos por dmigs 
[\[93b.5\]]pa med pa dang /_chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid du rtogs pa dang /_theg pa chen po gcig tu rtogs pa dang /_mi skye ba yongs su shes pa dang /_chos zab mo la nges par rtogs pa'i bzod pa khong du chud pa dang /_shes bya thams cad theg pa chen po'i thabs kyi sgo nas rab tu 
[\[93b.6\]]ston pa dang /_rtog pa thams cad gcod pa dang /_mtshan mar 'dzin pa'i rtog pa med pa dang /_'jig tshogs la lta ba la sogs pa lnga 'dor ba dang /_'dod chags la sogs pa nyon mongs pa spong ba dang /_zhi gnas sgom [#]pa dang /_shes rab dang thabs la mkhas par 
[\[93b.7\]]byed pa dang /_sems nye bar zhi ba dang /_gzugs la sogs pa la thogs pa med pa'i ye shes dang /_mngon par zhen pa'i gnas ma yin pa dang /_ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du dus mnyam du sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du 'gro ba dang /_gdul bya dang mthun par thams cad du bdag gi 
[\[94a\]]
[\[94a.1\]]lus rab tu ston pa zhes bya ba yongs su sbyong ba rnam pa nyi shu po 'dis kyang snga ma bzhin du sa bdun pa rtogs par 'gyur ba yin no/_/sems can kun yid shes pa dang /_/mngon par shes pas brtse ba dang /_/sangs rgyas zhing bzang sgrub [#]pa dang /_/yongs 
[\[94a.2\]]su brtag phyir sangs rgyas bsten/_/dbang po shes dang rgyal ba yi/_/zhing sbyong sgyu ma ltar gnas dang /_/bsams bzhin srid pa len pa dang /_/las ni rnam pa 'di brgyad bshad/_/ces bya ba ni/_sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi spyod pa ji lta ba bzhin shes pa dang /_
[\[94a.3\]]'jig rten gyi khams su rdzu 'phrul gyi mngon par shes pas brtse ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing rten gser la sogs pa'i ngo bor yongs su bsgyur ba dang /_rnam pa thams cad du chos yongs su brtag pas sangs rgyas mnyes par byed pa dang /_lha'i mig skyed [#]pa dang /_sangs 
[\[94a.4\]]rgyas kyi zhing rten [#]pa sems can yongs su sbyong ba dang /_thams cad du sgyu ma lta bur gnas pa dang /_sems can thams cad kyi don mthong nas blo sngon du btang [#]ba'i skye ba yongs su 'dzin pa zhes bya ba yongs su sbyong ba'i chos de lta bu rnam pa brgyad kyis snga ma bzhin du sa brgyad pa mi 
[\[94a.5\]]g.yo ba nyams su myong bar 'gyur ba yin no/_/smon lam dag ni mtha' yas dang /_/lha la sogs pa'i skad shes dang /_/spobs pa chu bo lta bu dang /_/mngal du 'jug pa mchog dang ni/_/rigs dang rus dang cho 'brang dang /_/'khor dang skye ba dag dang ni/_/nges 'byung byang

[\[94a.7\]]dang /_rgyal po la sogs par gnas pa dang /_nyi ma la sogs pa las skyes pa dang /_ma la sogs pa dang legs par 'brel pa'i gnyen dang /_rang dbang ba'i 'khor dang /_brgya byin la sogs pas mngon par bstod par skye ba dang /_sangs rgyas la sogs pas bskul te nges

[\[94b.1\]]par 'byung ba dang /_yid bzhin gyi [#]nor bu dang 'dra [#]ba'i shing a shwad tha [#]la sogs pa dang /[ #]sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi ngo bor gyur pa'i yon tan thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa zhes bya ba de lta bu'i phun sum tshogs pa'i mtshan nyid kyi yongs su sbyong ba rnam pa bcu gnyis pos[#]/_snga ma 
[\[94b.2\]]bzhin du yongs su sbyang ba byas pa'i khyad par dag gis sa dgu pa legs pa'i blo gros mngon du byed pa yin no/_/rgyu'i sa rnams kyi yongs su sbyong ba de ltar nges par bstan nas/_'bras bu'i sa nyid du de dag ma bshad par sa thams cad bsdu ba'i sgo nas sa bcu pa'i mtshan nyid/_sa dgu

[\[94b.4\]]'ong ba dang /_phyir mi 'ong ba dang /_dgra bcom pa nyid ces bya ba drug dang /_tshul gsum rnam par bzhag par dgongs pas bsdus pa lhag ma zhugs pa gsum dang /_rang sangs rgyas rnams [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du/_rigs dang /_brgyad pa dang /_mthong ba dang /_bsrabs 
[\[94b.5\]]pa dang /_'dod chags dang bral ba dang /_byas pa rtogs pa dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi sa rnams dang /_ji skad bshad pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i sa [#]rnam pa dgu po gcig kho na ste/_de ltar sa dgu 'das nas sa bcu pa la ni byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas

[\[95a\]]
[\[95a.1\]]rten can gnyis dang /_'dzin pa'i rnam par rtog pa gang zag rdzas su yod pa dang skyes bu btags [#]pa'i rten can gnyis so sor mthong ba'i lam dang bsgom pa'i lam la spong bas/_bzung [#]ba dang 'dzin par [#]rnam par rtog pa brgyad nye bar zhi bar bya ba'i phyir/_

[\[95a.3\]]mnyam nyid dang /_/sems can don dang 'bad med dang /_/mtha' las 'das par nges 'byung dang /_/thob pa'i mtshan nyid nges 'byung dang /_/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid dang /_/lam gyi yul can nges 'byung ste/_/rnam pa brgyad kyi bdag nyid 'di/_/nges 'byung bsgrub 
[\[95a.4\]]pa yin zhes bya/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_ched du bya ba ji skad bshad pa dang /_chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid dang /_sems can gyi don phyad par byed pa dang /_mtshan ma med par [#]thams cad byed pa nyid kyis lhun gyis grub pa dang /_rtag pa dang chad pa dang bral ba'i ngo bo'i gnas

[\[95a.7\]]cad mkhyen pa nyid rtogs pa ni lam shes pa nyid yongs su shes pa med na med pas lam shes pa nyid/_lha rnams rung bar bya ba'i phyir/_/'od kyis mog mog por mdzad dang /_/yul nges pa dang khyab pa dang /_/rang bzhin dang ni de yi las/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_lam 
[\[95b\]]
[\[95b.1\]]shes pa nyid skye bar rung ba nyid du bsgrub pa'i phyir/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rang bzhin gyi 'od dag gis lha la sogs pa'i rnam par smin pa'i 'od mog mog por mdzad pa ni nga rgyal bcom pa'i rgyud kho na la rtogs pa skye'o [#]zhes bya bar shes par bya ba'i phyir mdzad de/_de'i phyir tshig 
[\[95b.2\]]zur gyis rten brjod pa yin no/_/de yang byang chub tu sems bskyed pa kho na yin pas yul so sor nges pa yin no/_/theg pa gsum rnam par bzhag pa ni dgongs pa can yin [#]gyi/_mtshan nyid pa ni ma yin no zhes lugs las 'byung bas skye bo thams cad bla na med pa yang dag 
[\[95b.3\]]par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi mthar thug pa can yin te/_de'i phyir 'dod chags dang bral ba dang /_cig shos [#]kyi rnal 'byor pas kyang sangs rgyas nyid thob par bya ba'i phyir lam shes pa nyid bsgom par bya ba yin pas khyab pa yin no/_/byang chub tu sems bskyed pa ni 'khor ba 
[\[95b.4\]]ji srid par sems can gyi don byed par zhugs pa nyid kyis nyon mongs pa rnam pa thams cad du spangs pa ma yin pa zhes bya ba ni rang bzhin yin no/_/de'i 'og tu de lta bu'i rang bzhin can gyi las ni yang dag pa'i mtha' mngon du mi byed pa dang shes rab dang thabs la mkhas pas 
[\[95b.5\]]sems can yongs su ma bsdus pa yongs su sdud pa la sogs pa yin pas byed pa yin no/_/de ltar rten la sogs pa brjod nas lam shes pa nyid kyi skabs su lam thams cad rdzogs par bya dgos pas/_nyan thos kyi lam/_lam shes nyid kyi tshul la ni/_/'phags 
[\[95b.6\]]pa'i bden pa bzhi dag gi/_/rnam pa mi dmigs sgo nas ni/_/nyan thos lam 'di shes par bya/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_de la sdug bsngal gyi bden pa'i [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du/_mi rtag pa dang /_sdug bsngal ba dang /_stong pa dang /_bdag med pa ste/_de dag gi mtshan 
[\[95b.7\]]nyid zhi ba'i rnam pa bzhi dag dang /_kun 'byung ba'i bden pa'i rgyu dang /_kun 'byung ba dang /_rab tu skye ba dang /_rkyen gyi ngo bo nyid dang /_nad dang /_'bras dang /_zug rngu dang /_sdig pa'i rnam pa rnams dang /_yid 'byung ba dang /_'dod chags dang bral bar bya ba dang /_'gog 
[\[96a\]]
[\[96a.1\]]pa'i phyir zhugs pa yin no zhes 'byung ba'i phyir/_sdug bsngal dang kun 'byung gi bden pa de dag nyid so so'i [#]yid 'byung ba dag gi mtshan nyid kyi [#]pha rol dang 'jig pa'i chos can gyi rnam pa'i ngo bo nyid dag dang /_'dod chags dang bral ba dag gi ngo bo g.yo ba dang 
[\[96a.2\]]rab tu 'jig pa'i rnam pa dag dang /_'gog pa'i rang gi ngo bo 'jigs pa dang nad 'go ba dang 'tshe ba'i rnam pa rnams dang /_'gog pa'i bden pa'i 'gog pa'i rang gi ngo bo bdag med pa dang /_zhi ba dang gya nom pa'i rnam pa'i bdag nyid dben pa dang /_nges par 'byung ba'i mtshan nyid 
[\[96a.3\]]stong pa nyid dang mtshan ma med pa dang smon pa med pa dang mngon par 'du byed pa med pa'i rnam pa dag dang /_lam gyi bden pa'i lam dang /_rigs pa dang /_sgrub pa dang /_nges par 'byin pa'i rnam pa rnams/_ngo bo nyid mi dmigs pa'i sgo nas lam shes pa nyid kyi skabs su nyan thos 
[\[96a.4\]]rnams kyi lam yin par byang chub sems dpas de ltar yongs su shes par bya'o/_/bden pa bzhi yongs su shes pa ni/_nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun pa rtogs pa sngon du 'gro ba can yin pas lam brjod nas nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun pa/_'phags pa nyan thos lam 
[\[96a.5\]]la ni/_/gzugs la sogs pa stong pa'i phyir/_/stong pa nyid rnams dbyer med pas/_/dro ba de dag mi dmigs pas/_/rtse mor gyur pa dag tu 'dod/_/de la rtag la sogs tshul gyis/_/gnas pa bkag pas bzod pa rnams/_/sa bcu dag las brtsams nas ni/_/
[\[96a.6\]]mi gnas rgyas par bstan pa yis/_/chos kyi mchog tu 'gyur bshad de/_/ci [#]phyir zhe na sangs rgyas kyis/_/mkhyen nas chos rnams ma gzigs phyir/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_gzugs la sogs pa'i phung po rang rang gi ngo bo nyid kyis stong pa'i phyir stong pa nyid rnams 
[\[96a.7\]]phan tshun tha dad pa med pa dang /_gzugs la sogs pa snga ma bzhin du mi dmigs pa dang de bzhin du gzugs la sogs pa la rtag pa'ang ma yin mi rtag pa yang ma yin no zhes dmigs pa'i tshul gyis gnas pa bkag pa dang /_gang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs pas byang 
[\[96b\]]
[\[96b.1\]]chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas chos rnams ma gzigs pa zhes bya ba [#]tshad ma'i skyes bus ma [#]dmigs pa'i gtan tshigs kyi [#]'thad pas/_sa rab tu dga' ba la sogs pa la mi gnas pa rgyas par ji skad du bstan pa zhes bya ba'i rnam pa de rnams kyis/[ {]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin 
[\[96b.2\]]du bden pa'i dmigs pa la nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun pa skye bo zhes bya ba yin no/_/nyan thos kyi lam gyi mjug thogs su rang sangs rgyas kyi lam brjod par rigs mod kyi/_nyan thos rnams las de dag ji ltar khyad par [#]du 'phags na gang gis na de dag gi lam gzhan yin snyam 
[\[96b.3\]]pa'i dogs pa bsu bar re zhig khyad zhugs pa nyid du sgrub [#]pa/_rang byung bdag nyid rtogs [#]pa'i phyir/_/gzhan gyis bstan yang mi dgos la/_/bse ru lta bu'i ye shes ni/_/zab pa nyid du mngon par [#]brjod/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_lung las nyan thos rnams ni gzhan 
[\[96b.4\]]gyis bstan pa la ltos [#]nas rang gi byang chub rtogs par 'gyur la/_gzhan dag kyang brjod pa dang bcas pa'i chos bstan pas dge ba la 'jug par byed do zhes 'byung ngo /_/rang sangs rgyas rnams ni sngon thos pa la sogs pa mngon par 'dus byas pas gzhan gyis bstan pa la mi ltos [#]
[\[96b.5\]]par bdag nyid kyis rang gi byang chub mngon par rtogs par 'gyur te/_de'i phyir de dag la sangs rgyas la sogs pas bstan mi dgos so zhes bya ba ni khyad zhugs pa nyid gcig yin no/_/sgrar brjod pa'i chos bstan pa'i sgo nas nyan pa po rnams kyis 'chad pa po'i shes pa'i 
[\[96b.6\]]rtsal rtogs par byed na/_de dag ni rang gis thob pa'i ye shes la sogs pa'i mthus sgra med pa'i chos bstan pas/_gzhan dag dge ba bcu la sogs pa la 'jug par byed de/_de'i phyir de dag gi ye shes mi rtogs pa nyid ni khyad zhugs pa gnyis pa yin no/_/yang ji ltar

[\[97a.1\]]par g.yeng ba yin la/_de yang rgyud 'khrug par byed pa yin pas/_byang chub sems dpa' 'di ltar sangs rgyas su gyur nas brjod pa med par chos ston par gyur cig ces smon lam btab po/_/de bas na sangs rgyas dang 'dra ba'i phyir/_rang sangs

[\[101a.7\]]ba med pa'i rnam pa [#]mnyam pa nyid du rtogs pas/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams la nye bar gyur par 'dod pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa gang yin pa de ni/_shes rab kyis tshu rol gyi mtha' 'khor ba dang /_snying rjes pha rol gyi mtha' mya ngan las 'das pa ste/_
[\[101b\]]
[\[101b.1\]{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du rtag pa dang chad pa'i mtshan nyid dang de dag gi bar na yang mi gnas pas srid pa dang zhi ba la mi gnas pa yin no/_/kun shes pa nyid kyi skabs las bzlog ste bstan pas/_nyan thos la sogs pa la dus gsum mnyam pa nyid du shes pa med pa'i phyir/_yang 
[\[101b.2\]]dag pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang ring bar gyur pas rang gi rtogs pa tsam gyi shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa snying brtse ba dang shes rab dang bral ba'i phyir dngos po dang /_dngos po med par dmigs pa nyid kyis 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa la gnas pa yin par shes par 
[\[101b.3\]]bya'o/_/lugs las/_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba gang /_/de nyid khyod ni stong par bzhed/_/ces 'byung ba'i phyir/_dus gsum mnyam pa nyid du shes pa ni gzhi rtogs [#]pa kho na yin la/_de yang thams cad la yod pa nyid ma yin nam/_ji ltar na nyan thos dang 
[\[101b.4\]]byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa la yang dag pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ring ba dang cig shos su 'gyur zhe na/_de ni mtshan mar dmigs sgo nas/_/thabs ma yin pas ring ba ste/_/de ni thabs la mkhas pa yis/_/yang dag nye ba nyid du bshad/_/
[\[101b.5\]]ces bya ba gsungs te/_sgyu ma mkhan gyis sprul pa'i dngos po snang ba la de'i rang gi ngo bo mi shes pas dngos por mngon par zhen pa nyid kyis ngo bo nyid med par mi snang ba bzhin du/_dge ba'i bshes gnyen la sogs pa thabs mkhas pa dang bral ba'i phyir dngos po mtshan ma 
[\[101b.6\]]dang ldan par rtogs pas nyan thos la sogs pa dngos po'i ngo bo mi shes pa rnams la mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes [#]med de/_de'i phyir de dag la rgyal ba'i yum ring bar gyur to/_/byang chub sems dpa' dus ring mo zhig nas dge ba'i bshes gnyen yang dag par mgu 
[\[101b.7\]]bar byas pa'i man ngag phyin ci ma log pa can bden pa gnyis la rten [#]pa'i thos pa la sogs pa'i [#]skye ba'i thabs la mkhas pas/_dngos por mngon par zhen pa'i 'khrul pa'i mtshan ma bsal ba rnams la ni/_gzugs la sogs pa'i chos yongs su shes pa de nyid mnyam pa 
[\[102a\]]
[\[102a.1\]]nyid du yongs su shes pa yin te/_de'i phyir de dag la yum 'di yang dag par nye bar gyur pas/_thabs ma yin pas ni ring ba nyid yin la thabs kyis ni mi ring ba nyid yin no/_/de ltar nyan thos la sogs pa'i yum ring bar gyur pa'i bsgrub pa nyid mi 
[\[102a.2\]]mthun pa'i phyogs yin par/_gzugs sogs phung po stong nyid dang /_/dus gsum gtogs pa'i chos rnams dang /_/sbyin sogs byang chub phyogs rnams la/_/spyod pa'i 'du shes mi mthun phyogs/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_gzugs la sogs pa dus gsum pa'i 
[\[102a.3\]]chos zag pa dang bcas pa dang zag pa med pa dang /_gnyis ka'i ngo bo dngos por ma gyur pa thams cad la/_dngos por dmigs pa nyid kyis gzhan gyis kun brtags pa'i bdag la sogs pa stong pa nyid du lta ba dang /_sgrub pa'i 'du shes ni de dag gi gnyen po yin na yang phyin ci 
[\[102a.4\]]log tu zhugs pa nyid kyis spang bar bya ba yin pa'i phyir/_mi mthun pa'i phyogs yin no/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bzlog ste gnyen po yin par/_sbyin la sogs la ngar 'dzin med/_/gzhan dag de la sbyor byed pa/_/de ni chags pa'i mtha' 'gog pas/_/rgyal 
[\[102a.5\]]la sogs la chags pa phra/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_'khor gsum rnam par dag pas sbyin pa la sogs pa la bdag med par rtogs pas rang dang gzhan sbyor ba ni/_yang dag par zhugs pa'i phyir dang chags pa'i tshogs kyi gnas thams cad bkag pas blang bar bya ba yin 
[\[102a.6\]]pa'i phyir/_rnam pa thams cad du gnyen po yin no/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la sogs pa la phyag 'tshal ba la sogs pa ni bsod nams kyi tshogs kyi rgyu yin pa nyid kyis gnyen po yin mod kyi/_chags pa phra mo'i ngo bo nyid kyis rnam pa thams cad du ma yin pas mi mthun pa'i 
[\[102a.7\]]phyogs yin no//_//bam po gsum pa/_yang ji ltar chags pa phra mo mi mthun pa'i phyogs yin zhe na/_chos kyi lam ni rang bzhin gyis/_/dben pa'i phyir na de zab nyid/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_gang gi phyir chos kyi rigs rnams ngo bo nyid kho nas stong 
[\[102b\]]
[\[102b.1\]]pas/_de dag zab pa nyid yin pa de'i phyir/_de bzhin gshegs pa la dmigs pa yang mi mthun pa'i phyogs yin no/_/'o na ji ltar spang zhe na/_chos rnams rang bzhin gcig par ni/_/shes pas chags pa spong ba yin/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_gzugs la sogs 
[\[102b.2\]]pa'i chos thams cad rang bzhin gcig pa kho na ste/_'di lta ste rang bzhin med pa zhes shes pa dang shes bya mnyam pa nyid du gcig par yongs su shes pa ni chags pa spong ba yin no/_/yang ji ltar chos rnams rang bzhin gyis zab pa yin zhe na/_mthong ba la sogs bkag pa 
[\[102b.3\]]yis/_/de ni rtogs par dka' bar bshad/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_gang gi phyir rnam par shes pa thams cad kyis dmigs pa'i don bsal bas rang bzhin de rtogs par dka' ba nyid du bshad pa de'i phyir de zab pa nyid yin no/_/yang de ltar rtogs par dka' ba nyid ci yin 
[\[102b.4\]]zhe na/_gzugs la sogs par mi shes phyir/_/de ni bsam mi khyab par 'dod/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_gang gi phyir gzugs la sogs pa nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i rnam pa'i bar gyi rang bzhin yongs su mi shes pas bsam pa las 'das pa nyid du 'dod pa 
[\[102b.5\]]de'i phyir/_de ni rtogs par dka' ba nyid yin no/_/de ltar mi mthun pa'i phyogs la sogs pa brjod nas mjug bsdu bar/_de ltar kun shes nyid lugs la/_/mi mthun gnyen po'i phyogs dag gi_/rnam par dbye ba ma lus pa/_/ji skad bshad pa 'dir [#]shes bya/_/
[\[102b.6\]]zhes bya ba gsungs te/_thams cad shes pa nyid kyi skabs su de ltar bshad pa'i tshul gyis [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du nyan thos dang byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs dang gnyen po'i rab tu dbye ba shes par bya'o/_de ltar mi mthun pa'i phyogs la sogs pa brjod 
[\[102b.7\]]nas/_de dag rnam par bsgom pa'i sbyor ba gang yin zhe na/_sbyor ba[#]/_gzugs sogs de mi rtag sogs dang /_/de ma rdzogs dang rab rdzogs dang /_/de la chags pa med nyid la/_/spyod pa bkag pa'i sbyor ba dang /_/mi 'gyur byed po med nyid dang /_/bya dka' rnam 
[\[103a\]]
[\[103a.1\]]gsum sbyor ba dang /_/skal ba ji bzhin 'bras thob pas/_/'bras yod 'dod pa de dang ni/_/gzhan las rag las med gang dang /_/snang ba rnam bdun shes byed do/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_gzugs la sogs pa'i chos thams cad dang /_

[\[103b.1\]]su shes pa'i bzod [#]pa dang [#]rjes su shes pa ste/_de ltar skad cig ma bcu drug gi bdag nyid ni/[ #]thams cad shes pa nyid kyi skabs su mthong ba'i lam yin no/_/bden pa'i rnam pa gang yin zhe na/_gzugs rtag ma yin mi rtag min/_/mtha' las 'das dang rnam dag

[\[104b.7\]]pas de'i phyir de dag gi shes bya'i sgrib pa'i gnyen po nyid du bsgom pa'i lam zag pa dang bcas pa dang /_zag pa med pa gnyis yin pas lam rnam pa gsum mo/_/de la dang po la byed pa po med pa dang /_shes pa po med pa dang /_'pho ba med pa dang /_'dul ba med pa'i

[\[105a.1\]]rnam pa ste/_bzhi ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du lam la sogs pa'i mtshan nyid dag yin no/_/gnyis pa la rmi lam dang /_sgra brnyan dang /_mig yor dang /_smig rgyu dang /_sgyu ma'i rnam pa ste lnga ni/[ {]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du ngo bo nyid med pa dang /_ma

[\[105a.3\]]pa dang /_spros pa med pa dang /_rlom pa med pa dang /_mi g.yo ba'i rnam pa ste/_drug ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du shes bya'i sgrib pa kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /_rnam par byang ba dang /_nyon mongs pa'i bag chags dang /_gzugs la sogs pa'i spros pa dang /_rang gi

[\[105a.6\]]dang /_lam gyi bden pa dag ni rgyu yin la/_sdug bsngal dang 'gog pa'i bden pa dag ni 'bras bu yin par don gyi sgo nas bstan te/_kun 'byung ba dang /_lam dang /_sdug bsngal dang /_'gog pa'i bden pa la grangs bzhin du rnam pa brgyad dang zhes bya ba la sogs

[\[106a\]]
[\[106a.1\]]rnam pa gzhan gyis kun brtags pa'i byed pa po dgag pas/_rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyi rnam pa dang /_nges par 'byung ba'i rnam pa yul du 'khrul pa'i bdag nyid brtags pa dang /_mtshan nyid dang /_dus su 'khrul pa rnams dgag pas/_chos

[\[120a.1\]]sems kyis mngon par zhen pa'i gsos kyis yongs su gsos te/_de'i phyir yang dpe ma grub pas 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa ni mnyam pa nyid do/_/gnyis ka mnyam pa nyid du rnam par bsgom pa ni rang gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing du sangs rgyas 
[\[120a.2\]]su 'gyur bas/_de'i 'og tu sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa/_sems can 'jig rten de bzhin du/_/snod kyi 'jig rten ma dag pa/_/de la dag par bsgrub [#]pa yis/_/sangs rgyas zhing ni dag pa nyid/_/ces [#]bya ba gsungs te/_sems can dang snod kyi 'jig 
[\[120a.3\]]rten gyi bye brag gis/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam pa gnyis la [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du bkres pa dang skom pa dang /_rdo ba dang tsher ma la sogs pa nyid kyis ma dag pa gang yin pa de'i gnyen por lha'i nye bar spyad pa dang /_gser gyi sa phyogs la sogs pa dag pa bskyed pa'i sgo nas 
[\[120a.4\]]rnam par dag pa ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa'o/_/rang gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa bsgrubs [#]pa ni thabs mkhas pas skal ba ji lta ba bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa bya dgos pas thabs la mkhas pa/_yul dang sbyor ba can 'di ni/_/dgra rnams las 
[\[120a.5\]]ni 'das pa dang /_/mi gnas ji bzhin shugs dang ni/_/thun mong ma yin mtshan nyid dang /_/ma chags pa dang mi dmigs dang /_/mtshan ma smon lam zad pa dang /_/de yi rtags dang tshad med pa/_/thabs la mkhas pa rnam pa bcu/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_bar 
[\[120a.6\]]chad kyi chos las yang dag par rgal [#]bas lha la sogs pa'i bdud las 'das pa dang /_chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid rnam par bsgoms pas mi gnas par gnas pa dang /_smon lam grub pas gzhan gyi don sngon gyi shugs kyis 'phags [#]pa byed pa dang /_dka' ba 
[\[120a.7\]]spyod pa thams cad shin tu goms pa nyid kyis thun mong [#]ma yin pa dang /_chos dkar po rnam par dag pas chos thams cad la 'dzin pa med pa dang /_rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid kyis mi dmigs pa dang /_rnam par thar pa'i sgo mtshan ma med pa nyid kyis mtshan ma 
[\[120b\]]
[\[120b.1\]]med pa dang /_rnam par thar pa'i sgo smon pa med pa nyid kyis smon pa med pa dang /_dri ba sngon du btang ba can phyir mi ldog pa'i chos brjod pas phyir mi ldog pa'i rtags dang /_yul thams cad shes pa nyid kyis tshad med pa ste/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i yul rnam pa 
[\[120b.2\]]bcu mngon sum du bya ba la dus dang dus ma yin pa shes pa'i sbyor ba ni thabs la mkhas pa yin no/_/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i man ngag gi bstan bcos mngon par rtogs pa'i rgyan ces bya ba las/_skabs bzhi pa'i 'grel pa'o//_//rnam pa thams cad 
[\[120b.3\]]mngon par rdzogs par rtogs pa thob pa la/_rab kyi mthar phyin pa'i rtogs pa 'byung bas/_go sla bar bya ba'i phyir rtags la sogs pa bsnyad pa sngon du 'gro ba'i rtse mo'i mngon par rtogs pa/_rmi lam na yang chos rnams kun/_/rmi lam lta bur lta la sogs/_/rtse mor 
[\[120b.4\]]phyin pa'i sbyor ba'i rtags/_/rnam pa bcu gnyis dag tu bzhed/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_shin tu goms pa'i phyir rmi lam gyi gnas skabs na yang chos thams cad rmi lam dang 'dra ba la sogs par lta ba dang /_nyan thos la sogs pa'i sa la dga' ba'i sems mi bskyed 
[\[120b.5\]]pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa la sogs pa mthong ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa dmigs pa dang /[ #]chos ston pa'i sems skye ba dang /_dmyal ba la sogs pa'i sems can mthong ba sngon du 'gro ba rang gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing du ngan song spong ba 
[\[120b.6\]]rjes su dran pa dang /_grong khyer la sogs pa tshig pa rab tu zhi bar byed pa'i bden pa'i byin gyis brlabs grub pa dang /_gnod sbyin la sogs pa mi ma yin pa med par byed pa'i bden pa'i tshig grub pa dang /_rang nyid mngon par shes pas pha rol gnon pa'i dge ba'i bshes 
[\[120b.7\]]gnyen bsten pa dang /_rnam pa thams cad du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la slob pa dang /_chos thams cad la mngon par zhen pa med pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi byang chub la nye bar gyur pa zhes bya ba rnam pa bcu gnyis ni rtse mo'i mngon par rtogs pa thob pa'i gnas 
[\[121a\]]
[\[121a.1\]]skabs kyi khyad par gyi rtags yin no/_/de ltar rtags kyis mtshon pa'i rnam par 'phel ba rnam pa du zhig yod ce na/_rnam par 'phel ba/_'dzam bu gling gi skye bo snyed/_/sangs rgyas mchod pa'i dge la sogs/_/rnam mang du ni dper mdzad 
[\[121a.2\]]nas/_/rnam 'phel bcu drug bdag nyid do/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_'dzam bu'i gling la sogs pa stong gsum gyi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sems can snyed kyis [#]de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod pa bas lhag pa dang /_khyad par du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yid 
[\[121a.3\]]la byed pa dang /_mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob pa dang /_byang chub par bya ba dang byang chub par byed pa'i chos rnams mi dmigs pa dang /_dge ba bcu la sogs pa dang /_gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa thob pa las khyad par du 'phags pa dang /_lha'i ris 
[\[121a.4\]]thams cad nye bar 'ong ba dang /_bdud thams cad zil gyis gnon pa dang /_ston pa dang 'dra bar skye bo la mtshungs par gnas pa zhes bya ba bsod nams rnam pa brgyad dang /_yang rnam pa thams cad du thabs la mkhas pas bslab pa yongs su dag pa dang /_sangs rgyas 
[\[121a.5\]]kyi rigs can du 'gyur ba dang /_'bras bu sangs rgyas nyid 'thob [#]pa'i rgyu mtshan dang /_pha rol tu phyin pa'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs kyi sems mi skye ba dang /_gzugs la sogs pa dang ldan par rtogs pa'i sems rab tu mi skye ba dang /_pha rol tu phyin pa kun bsdus pa shes pa 
[\[121a.6\]]dang /_phun sum tshogs pa thams cad 'thob pa dang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la nye bar gyur pa zhes bya ba bsod nams gzhan rnam pa brgyad dang [#]/_me tog la sogs pas sangs rgyas mchod pa'i dge ba la sogs pa rnam pa mang po dper mdzad nas gong nas gong 
[\[121a.7\]]du khyad par du 'phags pa nyid kyis [#]gnas skabs bcu drug gi ngo bo nyid ni rnam par 'phel ba yin no/_/de ltar rnam par 'phel bas rnam par spel ba'i rang dbang du gyur pa mthar phyin pa'i mtshan nyid rnams ni nges par brtan [#]pa yin pas nges par brtan [#]pa/_kun mkhyen nyid 
[\[121b\]]
[\[121b.1\]]gsum chos rnams kyi/_/yongs su rdzogs pa bla med pa/_/sems can don yongs mi gtong ba/_/brtan [#]pa zhes ni mngon par brjod/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_de ltar yang dag pa'i thabs la mkhas pa'i stobs kyis rnam par mi rtog pa [#]rtogs pa'i gnas skabs na/_
[\[121b.2\]]snying rje chen po mngon du byed pa'i ngo bos sems can gyi don yongs su mi gtong ba'i mtshan nyid/_rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la sogs pa ji skad bshad pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa nyid gsum gyi chos sems bskyed pa la sogs pa gong du smos pa rnams yongs su rdzogs pa bla na 
[\[121b.3\]]med pa ni nges par brtan [#]pa yin no/_/de ltar nges par brtan [#]par byed pas nges par brtan par 'gyur [#]ba'i mtshan nyid ni sems kun du gnas pa yin pas sems kun du gnas pa/_gling bzhi pa dang stong dag dang /_/stong gnyis gsum dag dper mdzad nas/_/bsod nams mang 
[\[121b.4\]]po nyid kyis ni/_/ting 'dzin yongs su bsgrags pa yin/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_tshad srid pa ni srang tshad kyis gzhal bar nus so zhes bya ba'i tshul gyis gling bzhi pa la sogs pa stong gsum pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams rnams kyis [#]pha rol tu phyin pa la sogs pa thams cad 
[\[121b.5\]]kyi ngo bo nyid ting nge 'dzin gyi bsod nams dang ye shes kyi so sor dper mdzad nas de dag las khyad par du 'phags shing tshad las 'das pa'i bsod nams mang po nyid kyis ting nge 'dzin gyi mtshan nyid brjod pa ni sems kun du gnas pa yin no/_/rtags la sogs pa de dag ni 
[\[121b.6\]{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du dro ba la sogs pa nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun pa bzhi'i ngo bo yin par khong du chud par bya'o/_/nges par 'byed pa'i cha dang mthun pa'i 'og tu mthong ba'i lam yin te/_de la mi mthun pa'i phyogs gnyen po dang bcas pa rnam pa bzhi yod pas re zhig mi 
[\[121b.7\]]mthun pa'i phyogs gzung bar rnam par rtog pa gnyis po/_'jug pa dang ni ldog pa la/_/gzung ba'i rnam rtog so sor de/_/dgu yi bdag nyid ji bzhin yul/_/min pa'i bdag nyid dag shes bya/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_'jug pa dang ldog pa mi dmigs pa dang /_
[\[122a\]]
[\[122a.1\]]dmigs pa'i ngo bo nyid dag [#]la [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' dang nyan thos la sogs pa'i chos len pa dang 'dor ba'i sgo nas gzung ba'o zhes gzung bar rnam par rtog pa nyon mongs pa dang 'dra ba mi mthun pa'i phyogs su gyur pa yul gyi dbye bas 
[\[122a.2\]]so sor rnam pa dgu yod pa ni dngos po dang ma 'brel bar 'jug pa nyid kyis phyin ci [(]lag [,]log [)]tu snang ba'i phyir yul ji lta ba bzhin ma yin pa'i rang gi ngo bo dag tu shes par bya'o/_/de ltar gzung bar rnam par rtog pa'i phyogs gnyis bstan nas 'dzin par rnam par rtog pa gnyis po/_so 
[\[122a.3\]]so'i skye bo 'phags dbye bas/_/sems can rdzas dang btags [#]yod par/_/rnam rtog 'dzin pa dag tu 'dod/_/de dag so sor dgu bdag nyid/_/gal te gzung don de bzhin med/_/de dag gang gi 'dzin par 'dod/_/de ltar de dag 'dzin pa yi/_/ngo bos stong pa'i 
[\[122a.4\]]mtshan nyid do/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_so so'i skye bo dang 'phags pa'i gang zag dag gi [#{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du skyes bu rdzas dang btags par yod pa'i rten can gyi 'dzin pa dag yod pas 'dzin par rnam par rtog pa [(]gags [,]gegs [)]byed pa yin pa'i phyir mi mthun pa'i phyogs su 
[\[122a.5\]]gyur pa yul gyi dbye bas so sor rnam pa dgu yod pa ni/_gang gi tshe de dag gi yul gyi dngos por gyur pa gzung ba'i don dag de bzhin du gzung ba'i ngo bor yod pa ma yin zhes bya ba de'i tshe gang gi yang 'dzin pa ma yin te/_de'i phyir de dag ni 'dzin pa'i ngo bos dben pa'i ngo bo 
[\[122a.6\]]yin pas phyin ci log tu snang ba'i phyir/_yul ji lta ba bzhin ma yin pa'i rang gi ngo bo dag tu shes par bya'o/_/gzung ba'i rnam par rtog pa dang po 'jug pa'i rten can ji ltar rnam pa dgu yin zhe na/_ngo bo nyid dang rigs dang ni/_/lam ni yang dag 'grub pa dang /_/shes pa'i 
[\[122a.7\]]dmigs pa ma 'khrul dang /_/mi mthun phyogs dang gnyen po dang /_/rang gis rtogs dang byed pa dang /_/de yi las bya'i 'bras bu la/_/'jug pa'i phyogs kyi rten can gyi/_/rnam rtog de ni rnam dgur 'dod/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_dben pas dben pa ma rtogs pa'i 
[\[122b\]]
[\[122b.1\]]ngo bo nyid dang /_sa mi g.yo ba la sogs pa la 'jug pas sangs rgyas kyi rigs su nges pa dag_/[#]sgyu ma lta bur rtogs pas mthong ba la sogs pa'i lam yang dag par 'grub pa dang /_snang brnyan tsam du zad pas shes pa'i dmigs pa ma 'khrul ba dang /_yon tan dang skyon mthong 
[\[122b.2\]]ba sngon du 'gro ba'i blang bar bya ba dang /_dor bar bya ba nyid kyis gnyen po dang mi mthun pa'i phyogs dang dri ma thams cad dang bral ba nyid kyis rang gis rtogs pa dang /_dman zhing gya nom pa ma yin pa nyid kyis nyan thos la sogs pa'i sa thag sring bar byed pa dang /_bsam pa ji 
[\[122b.3\]]lta ba dang mthun par sprul pas sems can gyi don gyi las dang /_yang dag pa'i thabs la mkhas pa'i stobs kyis skye bo thams cad mya ngan las 'das pa la dgod par bya ba'i 'bras bu la skyon med pa nyid kyis gzung bar bya ba nyid du 'jug par bya'o zhes bya ba gzung ba'i rnam 
[\[122b.4\]]par rtog pa dang po 'jug pa'i phyogs kyi rten can rnam pa dgu po mthong ba'i lam gyi sbyor ba'i gnas skabs kyis [#]spang bar bya bar bzhed pa yin no/_/gzung ba'i rnam par rtog pa gnyis pa ldog pa'i rten can ji ltar rnam pa dgu yin zhe na/_srid dang zhi bar ltung ba'i phyir/_/
[\[122b.5\]]rtogs pa dman pa nyid dang ni/_/yongs su 'dzin pa med pa dang /_/lam gyi rnam pa ma tshang dang /_/gzhan gyi rkyen gyis 'gro ba dang /_/ched du bya ba log pa dang /_/nyi tshe ba dang sna tshogs nyid/_/gnas dang 'jug la rmongs pa dang /_/rjes la 'gro zhes bya ba la/_/
[\[122b.6\]]rnam rtog dgu yi bdag nyid 'di/_/ldog pa'i phyogs kyi rten can te/_/nyan thos la sogs yid las 'byung /_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa gang yang rung ba zhig tu ltung ba nyid kyis rtogs [#]pa dman pa nyid dang /_dge ba'i bshes 
[\[122b.7\]]gnyen thabs la mkhas pa dang bral ba nyid kyis yongs su 'dzin pa med pa dang /_shes bya'i sgrib pa mtha' dag gi gnyen po ma yin pa nyid kyis sgrub pa'i lam ma tshang ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa la sogs pa'i gdams ngag la ltos [#]pa dang bcas pa nyid kyis gzhan 
[\[123a\]]
[\[123a.1\]]gyi rkyen gyis 'gro ba nyid dang sems can thams cad kyi mchog nyid kyi [#]sems chen po nyid la sogs pa la mi 'jug pa nyid kyis ched du bya ba log pa dang /_nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa'i gnyen po nyid kyis nyi tshe ba'i lam gyi byed pa dang /_dmigs pa dang 
[\[123a.2\]]bcas pa nyid kyis 'bras bu dang po la sogs pa rtogs pa tha dad pa nyid dang /_ma rig pa'i bag la nyal ba thams cad ma spangs pa nyid kyis gnas pa dang 'gro ba mi shes pa dang /_theg pa chen po thams cad sdud par byed pa nyid kyis rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid 
[\[123a.3\]]mya ngan las 'das pa thams cad kyi mjug tu 'gro ba la nyes pa dang bcas pa nyid kyis [#]gzung bar bya ba ma yin pa nyid du ldog par bya'o zhes gzung bar rnam par rtog pa gnyis pa ldog pa'i phyogs kyi rten can nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi rgyud la blang bar bya ba nyid du 
[\[123a.4\]]'byung ba rnam pa dgu po byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi mthong ba'i lam gyi sems dang /_sems las byung ba 'jug pa'i gnas skabs kyis spang bar bya bar bzhed pa yin no/_/so so skye bo'i 'dzin pa'i rnam par rtog pa dang po skyes bu rdzas su yod pa'i rten can ji ltar 
[\[123a.5\]]rnam pa dgu yin zhe na/_'dzin pa dang ni 'dor ba dang /_/yid la byed dang khams rnams ni/_/gsum po dag dang nyer 'brel dang /_/gnas dang mngon par zhen pa dang /_/chos kyi dngos po btags pa dang /_/chags pa dang ni gnyen po dang /_/ji ltar 'dod 
[\[123a.6\]]bzhin 'gro nyams la/_/'dzin pa dang por shes par bya/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_kun rdzob tu sgyu ma bzhin du 'dzin pa dang gtong ba dang /_yang dag pa nyid du yid la mi byed par yid la byed pa dang /_chos nyid kyi khams gsum car dang nye bar 'brel pa dang /_stong 
[\[123a.7\]]pa nyid du mi gnas pas gnas pa dang /_dngos po la mngon par ma zhen pas thams cad la mngon par zhen pa dang /_rdzas su yod pa med pas chos thams cad btags pa dang /_de kho na nyid shes pa'i ma chags pas mngon par ma zhen pa sngon du 'gro ba'i chags 
[\[123b\]]
[\[123b.1\]]pa dang /_mnyam pa nyid sgom pa'i gnyen po nyid kyis gnyen po dang /_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yang dag par ma shes pa nyid kyis/_ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du 'gro ba thogs pa la don dam pa'i dngos por mngon par zhen pas 'dzin par rnam par rtog pa dang po rnam pa dgu 
[\[123b.2\]]mthong ba'i lam gyi sbyor ba'i gnas skabs kyis spang bar bya bar bzhed pa yin no/_/'dzin par rnam par rtog pa gnyis pa skyes bu btags par yod pa'i rten can ji ltar rnam pa dgu yin zhe na/_ched bzhin nges par mi 'byung dang /_/lam la lam min nges 'dzin dang /_/'gag 
[\[123b.3\]]dang bcas pa'i skye ba dang /_/dngos po ldan dang mi ldan dang /_/gnas dang rigs ni 'jig pa dang /_/don du gnyer dang rgyu med dang /_/phyir rgol [#]ba ni dmigs pa la/_/'dzin pa'i rnam rtog gzhan yin no/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_nyan thos la sogs pa'i nges par 'byung ba nyid 
[\[123b.4\]]kyis ched du bya ba ji lta ba bzhin du nges par mi 'byung ba dang /_rang gi mngon par 'dod pa'i lam med pas lam gzhan la lam ma yin par nges par 'dzin pa dang /_rgyu dang 'bras bu kun rdzob kyi ngo bos skye ba dang /_'gag pa dang /_bar chad med pa dang cig shos su snang ba nyid kyis 
[\[123b.5\]]dngos po mtha' dag ldan pa dang mi ldan pa dang /_nam mkha' la gnas pa'i bya dang 'dra ba nyid kyis gzugs la sogs pa la gnas pa dang /_byang chub kyi sems bskyed [#]pa la sogs pa'i sgo nas nyan thos la sogs pa'i rigs 'jig pa dang /_de bzhin nyid las khyad par du 
[\[123b.6\]]'phags pa'i chos med pas 'dod pa med pa dang /_don dam pa'i bden pa la brten nas rgyu med pa dang ser sna shin tu goms par byas pa'i chos nyid kyis bdud la sogs pa phyir rgol ba'i dngos po dmigs pa la btags pa'i dngos por mngon par zhen pa nyid kyis 'dzin pa'i rnam 
[\[123b.7\]]par rtog pa gnyis pa rnam pa dgu mthong ba'i lam gyi sems dang sems las byung ba 'jug pa'i gnas skabs kyis spang bar bya bar bzhed pa yin no/_/de ltar na bar skabs kyi tshigs su bcad pa dgus rnam pa bzhi nyid las so sor mthong ba'i lam gyi mi mthun pa'i phyogs 
[\[124a\]]
[\[124a.1\]]rnam pa dgu yin la/_de dag spang ba nyid du gnyen po rnam pa bzhi yang de bzhin du dbye bas tha dad do zhes bya bar khong du chud par bya'o/_/de ltar mthong ba'i lam la mi mthun pa'i phyogs gnyen po dang bcas pa bstan nas/_byang chub chen po 'grub par

[\[124a.3\]]te/_'chad par 'gyur ba'i mtshan nyid can gyi byang chub la gzhan dag mthong ba'i lam la sogs pa ston pas rab tu 'god pa ni gcig yin no/_/byang chub kyi rgyu mtshan kho nar de dag la yang dag pa'i tshig dang don la sogs pa'i sgo nas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yang dag par 
[\[124a.4\]]so sor gtong ba ni gnyis pa yin no/_/byang chub thob [#]par byed pa'i bar chad med pa'i rgyu shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rang gis sgom [#]pa la sogs pa'i bsod nams ches mang ba'i mtshan nyid ni gsum pa yin no/_/gang gi don du mthong ba'i lam rgyu de lta bu dang bcas par 
[\[124a.5\]]bzhed pa'i [#]byang chub chen po de gang yin zhe na/_bar skabs kyi tshigs su bcad pas byang chub chen po/_dri ma zad dang mi skye ba'i/_/ye shes byang chub ces brjod de/_/zad med skye med phyir de dag_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du shes par bya/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_nyon mongs pa 
[\[124a.6\]]dang shes bya'i sgrib pa'i dri ma skyes pa dang ma skyes pa nyid du brtags [#]pa rnams ni/_chos kyi dbyings las ma gtogs par/_/'di ltar chos yod ma yin te/_/zhes 'byung bas/_chos kyi dbyings kyi ngo bo nyid rnams nam mkha' bzhin du 'gag pa dang skye ba med pa'i 
[\[124a.7\]]phyir ram/_gcig dang du ma'i ngo bo nyid dang rgyu dang 'bras bu'i mtshan ma la sogs pas 'thad [#]pa'i dngos po med pas nam mkha'i pad+ma bzhin du 'gag pa dang skye ba med pa'i phyir [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du dri ma rnams zad pa dang mi skye ba'i shes pa chos thams cad phyin ci ma 
[\[124b\]]
[\[124b.1\]]log par rtogs pa'i mtshan nyid chos kyi sku la sogs pa ji lta ba bzhin gyi bdag nyid ni byang chub chen po yin par brjod do/_/gang gi phyir de lta yin pa'i [#]phyir/_gang dag dngos po 'jig pa'i bsam pas zad pa la zad do snyam pa'i shes pa dang /_dngos po mi skye ba'i bsam pas

[\[134a.5\]]pa med pas dam pa'i chos mnyan pa la sogs pa'i bzang po thob par mi 'gyur ro/_/ye shes kyi bdag nyid kyi chos kyi sku rnal 'byor pa so sor nges pa'i rgyud kyi rten la yod pa skad cig re re la 'byung ba ji ltar khyab pa dang /_rtag pa zhes kyang brjod ce na/_bar skabs

[\[134b.1\]]spyod rdzogs pa'i sku gzugs kyi sku'i ngo bo nyid mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos 'bar ba/_mtshan ni sum cu rtsa gnyis dang /_/dpe byad brgyad cu'i bdag nyid 'di/_/theg chen nye bar longs spyod phyir/_/thub pa'i longs spyod rdzogs skur bzhed/_/ces bya ba gsungs te/_mtshan

[\[135a.1\]]po bzhi bstan [#]pas phyag dang zhabs kyi sor mo ngang pa'i rgyal po lta bur dra [#]bar 'brel pa nyid dang /_bza' ba dang bca' ba la sogs pa gya nom pa byin pas phyag dang zhabs 'jam zhing gzhon sha chags pa nyid dang /_bldag pa la sogs pa shin tu gya nom

[\[139a.1\]]med pa la'o/_/de'i 'og tu sa bdun pa la thabs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i stobs kyis sems can yongs su smin par byed pa la'o/_/de nas stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i stobs kyis sa brgyad pa la nyan thos la sogs pa dang thun mong ma yin pa'i byang chub
